For your transcendental pleasure, over the past year commemorating your all-auspicious 108th appearance anniversary in 2004, your center at ISKCON Amravati has carried out extensive activities of book distribution, *harināma,* and Food for Life constantly in many villages around us. We will continue to do so. We just beg your mercy so that we may have the strength, right intelligence, and blessings to continually conduct these programs.

It is your causeless mercy that ISKCON Amravati now has five acres of its own land in Kondinyapur, which is the blessed birthplace of Śrīmatī Rukmiṇī Devī. We take this *saṅkalpa* at your lotus feet on your 109th appearance anniversary: that we will broadcast the sublime message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to thousands of people through our new center in Kondinyapur. You said, “Preaching is the essence,” and we want to please you by demonstrating our firm faith in your precious words — by acting on them. Please bless us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so that we may be able to please you and also please Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhīśa.

Your granddisciple servants at ISKCON Amravati, India.  
(written by Akrūra dāsa)

## Aravade

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to your Vyāsa-pūjā celebration!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, this past year we held special celebrations in honor of the 108th anniversary of your birth (8 September 2004). Until now, millions of people have taken advantage of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, which was spread by you all over the world. You fulfilled the intense desire of Lord Caitanya to spread the holy name all over the world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your transcendental pleasure this offering is a progress report from ISKCON’s center in Aravade, a remote village of a few thousand residents. Here we have plans for a Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopāla *mandira,* a Kṛṣṇa-tattva Darshan (diorama), a yearly one-week *padayātrā*(*ḍiṇḍi-yātrā*), and many other activities.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, kindly bless us so that all the ISKCON devotees here in Aravade nicely follow all the rules and regulations you set up. Only by pleasing you and your representatives will we be able to advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Before your departure back to Godhead, you blessed us with your instructions to your dear disciples about their services. Śrīla Prabhupāda, your disciples have been preaching in villages and through Padayātrā in India and all over the world. Once when you were in Bombay, Lokanāth Swami presented a report to you about the preaching in Aravade; he told you that “a devotee from Aravade is chanting sixteen rounds and following the four regulative principles.” That report inspired you, and you immediately said, “Make him in charge of that preaching place.” That was your compassion on me.

Devotees took your instruction as a blessing and started preaching sincerely here. Since that day, by your empowering blessings this center has taken good shape. We have three and half acres of land, on which we plan to build a medium-size temple of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopāla within two years. In this temple project we are planning a beautiful altar for Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopāla, a large *kīrtana* hall, a bookstall, a life membership office, and a reception area. We are on a marathon to accomplish this temple. Recently we have completed construction of a building for a diorama exhibition named “Kṛṣṇa-tattva Darshan,” in which we will have exhibits on different subjects based on your teachings — especially *Śrīmad Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* — and ISKCON worldwide. This spiritual educational exhibition is especially intended for high school and college students from our area.

During the past year commemorating the 108th anniversary of your birth, as a special offering to you our preaching team has been inspired to preach to many youths in many colleges, as well as to present courses on the *Bhagavad-gītā*. We are running five weekly *sat-saṅga* programs for congregations.

This year will see our third annual walk on Ḍiṇḍi-yātrā, a large regional walking pilgrimage, with *harināma-saṅkīrtana* and book distribution. We will be walking toward Pandharpur and plan to reach that town on Ekādaśī or before.

We have opened an extended preaching center in the nearby city of Sangli, where weekly programs are held each Sunday for our congregation and each Saturday for children, and where ladies’ *sat-saṅga* meetings are also regularly going on.

All the enrolled members from our life membership program are connected to Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We’ve held two successful prison preaching programs, which have been very well received by the prison staff. They see that the prisoners become happy by taking part in the congregational chanting of the holy name.

Festivals are conducted regularly. In December 2004, more than 15,000 pious people attended our sixth annual Hare Kṛṣṇa Utsava celebration and honored *prasādam*.

Our daily morning program and *brahmacārī sādhana* is being held to a good standard.

We are students, learning from your teachings how to co-operate, how to work with unity in diversity.

We pray that something out of this report is pleasing to you. This would be the success of our lives. We know that all our efforts are possible only by your causeless mercy upon us.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us such a wonderful Society of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, which has the potency to completely dispel the darkness of this material world and give us the ultimate goal of life, love of Godhead, *kṛṣṇa-prema*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!!

Your granddisciples at ISKCON Aravade, Sangli, Maharashtra, India.

## Austin

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances in the dust of your most sacred lotus feet.

I’m in Kaunas, Lithuania. It’s Friday, April 15, 2005. This is the cut-off date for submitting this year’s homage for your Vyāsa-pūjā book. My back is against the wall. I must either write and submit it now or it will not make it into the book. I’m just starting to recover from a heavy bout with fever and bronchitis that hit me two days ago when I arrived here to preach for several days to the wonderful devotees at your ISKCON Kaunas center. Encouraging my recovery, the weather has turned from gray to sunny. It’s the first warm, pleasant day I’ve seen since I arrived in the Baltic region over two weeks ago.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I wouldn’t trade this life you have given me for any other. I know I am the most undeserving and the most unqualified, but yet, somehow or other, you have empowered me to act as your representative. You have facilitated my traveling far and wide for spreading your supremely glorious teachings and for encouraging and being encouraged by your dedicated followers. I never dreamed that such a sublime life could be experienced anywhere within this miserable world. Now by your sublime grace it has become a living reality.

I bow forever at your lotus feet in humble gratitude,

Your followers at ISKCON Austin (ISKCON Cyberspace), Texas, USA.  
(written by Saṅkarṣaṇa dāsa Adhikārī)

## Baha (Bali)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Please allow us to briefly describe our Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurāṅga Ashram. On land that belonged to your Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, the temple was established in the year 2000 under the patronage of the Harinama Sankirtan Foundation. Located on an area covering 0.58 hectares, the temple is still very simple, with an earthen floor and a roof made of *kuśa* grass. We now have four full-time *brahmacārīs* staying at our ashram, and our congregational members number 64 families comprising about 200 devotees.

Our daily activities in the ashram are mostly in the form of *arcanā,* with regular *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class. For our congregational members, we conduct programs on Thursdays and Sundays. Class on the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* is given mostly by Vidura dāsa Prabhu, while for the *Bhagavad-gītā* we have different second-initiated devotees giving class. The chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* on beads is held for 15–30 minutes, guided by your *japa* tape. We also have a *gurukula* program for our children every Sunday.

On the Pūrṇimā day every month, devotees perform *parikramā* around the temple area with the Deities of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurāṅga. We do the *parikramā* before moonrise, followed by the *sandhyā-ārati* ceremony. Then we have *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class on the story of Dhruva Mahārāja, verse by verse in sequence.

During the Balinese Hindu festivals, we perform *saṅkīrtana* by selling your books, as well as cassettes, incense, etc., at the nearest local temples. We are planning to renovate the temple, enlarging it from 9 m x 15 m to 14 m x 23 m. It will be done gradually, starting in July of this year. The site plan and the master plan for the entire project are complete. We have adopted, as far as possible, the principles of *vastu*. The total cost of the project will be approximately US$55,225.75. We have printed pamphlets and other publications about our Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurāṅga temple, and hopefully we may soon get the necessary funds to finish the entire project.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please be merciful unto us. Please always guide us so that we may always be nicely engaged in your devotional service. May your fame and glories be spread all over the three worlds.

Your devotees at the Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurāṅga temple in Baha, Bali, Indonesia.  
(written by Vidura dāsa)

## Bhakti-latā Purī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and respectful obeisances to your divine lotus feet.

On your Vyāsa-pūjā day the devotees here at the farm in Argentina want to thank you for giving us the opportunity to belong to this large and beautiful family of Kṛṣṇa devotees.

In a place as low as this, where the consumption of meat is a national tradition, we are very fortunate to have the chance to engage in your service. Argentina is a good place to preach, since people need Kṛṣṇa consciousness so much.

And that is our mission: to keep striving to spread this *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s so that everywhere the chanting of the holy names will be heard: Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

In the Marcos Paz farm we are making a great effort to finally develop a community of devotees that is an example for the whole society of how to live in a simple way with high thinking — that is, to live in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We know it’s not easy, but we also know that if we follow your divine instructions we’ll please you and Kṛṣṇa, and your mission will be successful.

We grow organic wheat on the farm, we are making maximum use of all the usable land, as you wanted, we make excellent flour, and we’re trying to establish a business based on this flour to generate income for the farm. All this is due only to your mercy.

Just as a child offers his father something insignificant and the father accepts it due to love for his son, we ask you to accept our offering. We come from very low families and are full of bad habits, but please accept our humble offering to your lotus feet. It goes with all our love, enthusiasm, sincerity, and humility.

The devotees at the Bhakti Latā Purī“Govinda Farm,” Marcos Paz, Buenos Aires province, Argentina.

## Bhaktivedanta Manor (UK)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Many years ago you sat on a park bench. A man who happened to sit beside you asked you about your mission. You replied that you had temples, schools, farm projects, restaurants, and thousands of devotees around the world. Only time separated you from them. In this way you demonstrated your divine vision for the future of ISKCON. Similarly, you had such a vision for Bhaktivedanta Manor.

During your time here you planted the seed for what, after thirty-two years, has grown into a blossoming lotus with abundant petals. You have inspired your disciples and granddisciples throughout the years to have the patience, fortitude, and commitment to help your vision become a reality and grow into the attractive manifestation that it is today.

Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda are the golden whorl of this lotus — the heart of this manifestation. Since you installed them so lovingly in 1973, the standard of worship has increased year after year in its opulence and magnificence. On their installation day, as you performed the *ārati* ceremony, the temple room became transformed into the spiritual realm. As the *kīrtana* swelled in a series of crescendos, you motioned for the devotees to jump higher. They jumped in spiritual ecstasy, feeling the connection you had with the Deities — prepared to jump to any heights to please you.

Our first campaign — and the first stage of growth — was to distribute your books throughout the British Isles. You also told us to not mind the weather and to hold programs in homes and schools, perform *harināma,* stage festivals, and also distribute *prasādam*. And you told us that theater would conquer Britain.

But for some years after your departure the growth was occasionally slow. At one point there remained only a handful of devotees. So a period of renewed commitment to growth for the lotus flower of Bhaktivedanta Manor began.

The second campaign was therefore to invite congregational members who had been practicing to become full-time ashram residents. Book distributors invited all people they met to become connected; thousands of letters and sweets and magazines flooded the country. Preachers visited the aforementioned congregational members in their homes. Almost one hundred of them came forward to become the next phase of growth.

By Lord Kṛṣṇa’s arrangement, since you had established the worship of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda, this part of England had become home to one of the largest communities of Indian families outside India. You said that all of the Manor’s financial problems would be solved if each family would donate one pound every month.

The third growth spurt here was when enthusiastic young preachers established youth clubs in London and went door-to-door with thousands of Kṛṣṇa conscious newspapers. When young Gujarati men and women came forward to take your teachings seriously, it led to the growth of the Life Membership department, which grew into the Indian Community Affairs Programme and then the Bhaktivedanta Manor Foundation.

With their industry and determination, the celebration of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s appearance has grown from a mere 250 visitors and a few stalls on the front lawn to one hundred times that number today.

As your lotus of Bhaktivedanta Manor grew, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the various petals that would one day make up the full bloom gradually became manifest — each petal a unique activity of devotional service, each connected to the divine center.

When you first conceived of ISKCON, you mentioned that it would be an organization to teach spiritual values and techniques to correct the imbalance of modern life. Now from the work with youth at the Manor has grown the university preaching, reaching into fifteen universities with your unique message. Tens of thousands of school and college students come here to learn something of Vedic culture and philosophy, and teams of devotees go out across London to hold classes. In addition, over 20,000 schoolchildren visit every year to learn more about the spiritual tradition of India.

You asked that the Manor should have a herd of cows, and now the cow protection program has grown to be perhaps the finest such project in the ISKCON world.

You wanted modern society to have access to Vaiṣṇava *brāhmaṇas* who would teach, preach, and preside over ceremonies in which people would be reminded of the Supreme Lord. Now one hundred young couples every year choose to have ISKCON *brāhmaṇas* perform their marriages, and thousands of ordinary people who would never enter a temple of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa have been touched by the holy name, the message of the *Gītā*, and *mahā-prasādam*.

You wanted a Kṛṣṇa-centered educational system, and here at Bhaktivedanta Manor we have a lively school where the pupils are taught each day about Lord Kṛṣṇa. We are committed to education, and the Kṛṣṇa club and youth club also provide opportunities for many young Vaiṣṇavas to learn and associate with one another.

You asked for the message of the *Gītā* to be recited in homes, and from Bhaktivedanta Manor the message continues to be discussed and practiced in countless homes across London and the south of England in *saṅgha* groups.

You wanted the philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to come alive through the theater, and our Bhaktivedanta Players enliven and enrich the lives of the audiences they entertain here at the Manor and abroad.

You commented that “Here at Bhaktivedanta Manor, the place is the nicest possible and the village is neat and clean” and encouraged us to maintain the grounds. Over the years we have planted hundreds of trees, hedgerows, shrubs, and flowers.

The lotus of Bhaktivedanta Manor has many petals, and just as those individual petals are growing, so new petals are developing too. In time they will increase the size, complexity, and beauty of your lotus here. No one petal is more important than the others, for a single petal can never be the entire flower. Indeed, the many petals must all grow to fullness for the lotus to preserve its symmetry, an important dimension of its complete beauty. Yet each petal must remain connected to the center, from which its nourishment comes and which is the storehouse of nectar.

You had the vision of your movement being the lotus of good fortune for Kali-yuga. You were the gardener. You walked here, thus making the soil fertile, then placed the seed of the lotus here. By your grace, may the Bhaktivedanta Manor lotus of good fortune continue to grow and spread its sweet fragrance throughout every town and village in the south of England. Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your servants at Bhaktivedanta Manor, England, UK.

## Boston

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humblest obeisance at your lotus feet, so uniquely significant to this place.

The loving grace of Kṛṣṇa is seen most explicitly in the *avatāra,* in which the Lord reveals Himself “foot to Earth.” As He came from Goloka to Bhauma, so you came from East to West. The unique feature of our city of Boston is that you first set foot on American soil here — an event that brought tears to the eyes of the entire *sampradāya.*

Surely countless tributes to you composed at this place since then have referred to this fact, attempting to describe what it means to one in this place now. Our community finally seized on the uniqueness of the event three years ago when conceiving of a large festival on the anniversary of your arrival, offering its exclusive gift to greater ISKCON in exchange for their association and participation in your glorification. In conceptualizing and advertising the gathering, we explored several interpretations of its significance, and our understanding of the festival has evolved over the last few years.

“The First Western Tīrtha” seemed to stick as a fairly straightforward slogan to summarize this meaning. It is truly accurate, in that this place was blessed by the mobile *tīrtha,* you, and thus consecrated when washed by those sampradāyic tears. The awkward truth that tugs on this motto, however, is that you kept moving. Boston was only a stopover, and on to New York you went. A cultural anthropologist might see in this the beginning of Boston’s competition with New York, or rather, on the larger world stage, its continuation.

New York went on to have one of the most successful and long-running Ratha-yātrās. New York has your Matchless Gifts storefront. New York is that city known the world over, and if *anything* happens it happens there. But now, thirty plus years later, we in Boston were to stake our claim, make our mark, be put on ISKCON’s festival map, start taking back our place in the sun and reverse the curse: we were the first!

We rode a boat in the harbor you came into. We had *kīrtana* in commemoration and glorification of your mission. We bathed a *mūrti* of you on the pier. We indeed felt your presence. We indeed missed you sorely. We became enthused again to practice your presence in our daily lives. But we had not yet confronted, as your disciples, what you confronted in that harbor in 1965.

Surely the writers of homages also mentioned how you wrote your now-famous “Mārkine Bhāgavata-dharma” while docked here. In this they located a clue to envision the significance of this festival in its maturity.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, at Commonwealth Pier you met, at a challenging journey’s end, the true challenge for which you set out. You confronted, alone, the possibility of mission. The concrete connection with this foreign place struck you and led you deep into the yearning for Kṛṣṇa’s hand to guide you in one thing: to transform, with God’s personal grace, the lives of anyone willing to hear you. To give them *akhila-rasāmṛta-mūrti* Kṛṣṇa, Rūpa Gosvāmīs Kṛṣṇa, Lord Caitanya’s Kṛṣṇa. You confronted that blessed possibility of revolution, and you confronted it here at the early edge of America known for revolution.

Now this arrival festival is seeing hope in the form of doing what you first did here. We will chant and we will dance and we will feel your presence. But we will also gather in Boston and confront, together, the challenges, strategies, and experiential grace in the possibility of mission.

Our Harvard forefather William James is quoted as saying, “One cannot come in contact with the living God and not be transformed.” Your followers know this to be true. Thank you for being the living proof of God’s living transformative grace and making real to us the opportunity to share Kṛṣṇa’s grace with others. Please bless us that in this task we may also be proof of that grace.

In separation from you, wishing to be your living instruments,

Your servants at ISKCON Boston, USA.  
(written by Vṛndāvana dāsa Ṭhākura dāsa)

## Botswana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

In the year 1996, by your mercy and the hard work of Krishna dāsa Swami from the UK, we were able to install large Deities of Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma in Botswana. The ceremony was carried out under the supervision of Krishna dāsa Mahārāja and Girirāj Mahārāja.

In the same year, we started the Food for Life program, which is still continuing to this day. Because the present temple facility is insufficient, we are about to construct a brand new, bigger temple. The plans have been officially approved, and construction will start this year.

When we take *darśana* of Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāmajī, you are always in our thoughts and prayers. If it were not for you and your wonderful disciples, who are continuing with the mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we would still be sleeping on the lap of Māyā.

Besides you, Krishna dāsa Mahārāja has been the main inspiration in our spiritual lives, and for over ten years now we have been performing the Gītā-jayantī*mahā-yajñas*. Who would have thought that in a remote corner of Africa Kṛṣṇa consciousness would be actively practiced? The desert in Botswana is called the Kalahari. Krishna dāsa Mahārāja has called it Kala Hari *dhāma* because of our Lordships Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue to shower your mercy on us so that we may grow to become a beautiful lotus poised high in the desert of Kalahari, untouched by the waters of Māyā. Thank you for all that you have given us.

Your fallen servants in Botswana, Africa.

## Brisbane

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to you on this, the most auspicious 109th anniversary of your divine appearance in this world!

Here we are in the world of 2005 — we, your aspiring servants, struggling to remain relevant: struggling to maintain purity of purpose and purity of comportment, struggling to keep our head in the clouds, receiving divine instructions, and our feet firmly on the ground, implementing them.

Struggling to be neither isolationists nor conformists. Struggling to be in the world but not of it. Struggling to bring succor to a world gone completely mad. Struggling to help ourselves and to help others.

Bring the bling, O Sarasvatī-devī! Give us the *alaṅkāra* of unbreakable determination, infuse us with the courage to step up to the plate and once again say to the world: “We are the representatives of God! We set the standards! We create the program!” Imbue us with the extreme personal humility to stand up again when we get smacked down.

Generations hence will study your personality to understand the full manifestation of all the good qualities of a saintly person and authentic leadership character. You never wavered, never negotiated your fundamentals. When you were knocked down, you got right back up. Whether you were gored by a bull or floored by a heart attack, nothing was going to stop you.

Please burn up all our impurities in the fire of devotional service. Give us the strength to keep ourselves in that fire until we are completely purified, even though the false ego burns all the while. Make us an instrument of your will, to understand your heart and realize your priorities.

*guru-mukha-padma-vākya, cittete kariyā aikya,*  
*āra nā kariha mane āśā*

Let us make your instructions one with our hearts and never desire anything else. Let us become exclusively focused on realizing your will, *vyavasāyātmikā-buddhi*. Let us accept you as the captain of the ship. You are not a sectarian religious figure or the focus of a personality cult; you are the embodiment of universal principles, a pure transmitter of divine vision and will.

Let us have firm determination to do this. And please, we beg you to give us the strength to carry it out, because we are weak and unable to overcome this fearsome material energy that is oppressing us.

Your aspiring servants in Brisbane, Australia.

## Budapest

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances on this holy and auspicious day, the anniversary of your appearance. All glories to Śrī Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

Although you are not here physically, we feel your presence every day. This is what gives us the power to fight against Māyā again and again and try to save the fallen souls here in Budapest. We meditate on your great personality, the endless patience and humility you showed as you strove to change the Western *mlecchas* and untouchable people into Vaiṣṇavas. We are trying to continue what you started and trying to remember you always and everywhere.

Your broad, loving smile comprises the devotees, who by giving up sense gratification are able to render devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa every day.  
Your strict glance comprises our spiritual masters and spiritual leaders, who help us maintain our enthusiasm day by day.  
Your hardworking, helpful hands are the book distributors, who do not shrink from any effort to elevate the fallen souls.  
Your cleanliness and punctuality are the *pūjārīs,* who are always engaged in Deity worship and also engage others.  
Your legs are strong pillars, which are the devotees who manage the *yātrā*.  
Your all-pervading mercy comprises the devotees distributing *prasādam,* who help people attain a more pious state by distributing food offered to Kṛṣṇa.  
Your perfect intelligence comprises the devotees of the only Vaiṣṇava Institution of Higher Education in Europe.  
Your fighting spirit comprises the devotees who are ready to defend you by even opposing the leaders of countries.  
Your renunciation comprises the Vaiṣṇava boys, who by your mercy got back their intelligence for self-realization.  
Your ethical behavior comprises the Vaiṣṇava ladies, who got back their honor, virtue, and real beauty by serving you, and whom we therefore respect as our mothers.  
Your pure mind comprises the temples, transcendental places that give shelter to the tortured souls of the Kali-yuga.  
Your heart is the Deity, the form of Kṛṣṇa through which He accepts our worship.  
Your body is ISKCON.

Please give us your mercy so we can always remember your form and always act as your limbs here in Budapest.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your insignificant servants in Budapest, Hungary.

## Buenos Aires

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

My most dear father Śrīla Prabhupāda,

First of all I would like to ask you to please accept what I am trying to offer as my most humble and respectful obeisances.

On this 109th anniversary of your glorious birth, once again I find myself with the honor of being able to glorify you.

Since I was young I have had the good fortune of being with the devotees, of belonging to this great family that you formed with the most pure and sincere love for God. This is a family of saintly Vaiṣṇavas filled with joy, enthusiasm, and bliss — hundreds and thousands of persons who dedicate themselves daily to devotional service. And it is all due to your compassion and your determination to follow the order of your spiritual master. Someday I would like to have just a fraction of the compassion and determination you have.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, there is no doubt that you are the greatest exponent of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the Occident. Not only did you expound it, but you also practiced it and lived it with us. All your life you prepared yourself to cross seas and continents and finally arrive at this distant place, so full of anxiety, illusion, and passion, so devoid of culture, values, and decency. You offered us the greatest gift — the sweetest nectar and highest transcendental knowledge — and we have taken it from your saintly hands.

What would have become of all of us without your mercy, dear Grandfather? What would have become of me without your perfect books? What would my life be without Kṛṣṇa consciousness? You did so much for us, and are doing so much for me, every day, every moment — because everything you taught us was spiritual, because everything you spoke was transcendental wisdom.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, from the bottom of my heart, for giving us so much, for being a perfect father, for understanding our faults and enthusing us to improve ourselves. Thank you for letting us form part of the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya. Thank you for instructing us in a practical and blissful way. Thank you for leaving so many sons, who have melted completely in the loving ecstasy your presence emanates, and who continue to take care of us and help us remain fixed in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the only way we can return home.

I am sorry for not serving you as you deserve, but at least I will try to always be near my godbrothers and -sisters and -nephews and -nieces, serving them and helping in whatever way I can, knowing that with your mercy and all my effort I may fulfill some of your desires. And in this way I hope to go back to that place I never should have left, the place where, without a doubt, you are waiting for us with the sweetest of smiles.

Your servants in Buenos Aires, Argentina.  
(written by Mūrti Mūrti-devī dāsī)

## Christchurch

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your ISKCON movement!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, where would we be without you? At such an advanced age you came to the fallen Western countries, taking up the task of personally saving the ignorant, misdirected products of Kali-yuga. Although superficially it appeared you arrived in New York City alone, you brought the entire disciplic succession and commanded all the potency required to fulfill the prophecy of Lord Caitanya:

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

**“In as many towns and villages as there are on the surface of the earth, My holy name will be preached.”**

You also write,

I think that His Divine Grace Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura is always seeing my activities and guiding me within my heart by his words. As it is said in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, tene brahma hṛdā ya ādi-kavaye*. Spiritual inspiration comes from within the heart, wherein the Supreme Personality of Godhead, in His Paramātmā feature, is always sitting with all His devotees and associates. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* Concluding Words\]

And today, although we have been without your physical association for almost three decades, still that potency is here, still devotees feel themselves personally saved by you.

New Zealand is viewed as a “clean green” oasis, with modern amenities widely available to all, and a liberal social welfare system that supports even the dregs of society. But the youth are frustrated, empty, angry, their only shelter the four pillars of sinful life. New Zealand has the highest youth suicide rate in the world. Clearly we are a stark example of how material opulence cannot satisfy the self. This is the nature of illusion. It appears to be one thing — a beautiful country — but the reality is quite different. Beneath the surface New Zealand is plagued with as many problems as any other society with its foundation in the ignorance of atheism.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, some of these children of Kali have been picked up by your servants and are now finding a sense of identity and direction within the shelter of the *saṅkīrtana* movement. We are growing up in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, trying to take more responsibility for your precious Society, and striving to clean ourselves of the heavy contamination of gross degradation from an early age.

Here we are developing many projects aimed at attracting the minds and hearts of the youth of Christchurch City. This involves long-term commitment to the individuals who are joining your Society. Now more than ever they require immediate care, ongoing spiritual nourishment, solid training, and security. Of course, they will not always be youthful; therefore we have to consider the future. Therefore we are endeavoring to create a situation in which, as the individuals change ashrams (as most will), they can have the required spiritual and material stability while keeping the essence of directly contributing to the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

By your mercy, we now have a vibrant inner-city preaching center, which functions as a restaurant during the day. At night we teach yoga and meditation. These classes include a full meal of *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*. Saturday night is our “Loft Night.” This program has been specifically designed for those experiencing Kṛṣṇa consciousness for the first time. Our motto is “We make it easy to come to Kṛṣṇa,” but we don’t compromise our principles. Our devotee staff is trained, the atmosphere open and friendly, the *kīrtana* sweet, and the *prasādam* a taste sensation never to be forgotten. Often guests will come carrying your precious books, Śrīla Prabhupāda, wanting to learn more.

In addition, devotees are engaged in book distribution, *harināma-saṅkīrtana,* Deity worship, festival planning, Sunday Feast programs, and congregational preaching, as well as caring for their families and raising their children in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

The devotees here pray that more and more people will come forward to help in the endeavor to soundly establish your ISKCON Society here in Christchurch and all over the world.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, disregard our whimsical plans for self-satisfaction and engage us fully in your work.

*Patita-pāvana* Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your young and old granddisciples at ISKCON Christchurch, New Zealand.  
(written by Keli Muralī-devī dāsī)

## Coventry

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O most merciful Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

We at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa Cultural Center in Coventry wish to pay tribute to the most special, worshipable personality the modern world has ever known.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, all your efforts were made for the world’s population to become Kṛṣṇa conscious, and your greatest pleasure was to hear how your disciples were continuing with the tradition of preaching.

Although all the devotees in the Coventry *yātrā*are in the *gṛhastha āśrama* and are tied up with *gṛhastha* duties, we are still attempting to preach to people at all times by dressing in Vaiṣṇava clothes, preaching extensively, and generally behaving in a devotee manner. At this point it is important to stress that by your mercy the all-round mood of the temple is extremely devotional, a mood being encouraged by our Guru Mahārāja, Krishna dās Swami, and practically implemented by the temple management. By your mercy the devotees in this temple, which is a small place, have been able to interact in such a way that backbiting, unfriendliness, and one-upmanship have been virtually eliminated, and greater cooperation has taken their place. This has happened because everyone here is genuinely trying to follow in your footsteps.

Indeed, Śrīla Prabhupāda, although the temple is small, this past year the devotees still managed to stage a wonderful drama called *Kṛṣṇa of Vṛndāvana*, attracting nine hundred devotees to a professional theater to celebrate the tenth anniversary of the installation of our wonderful Deities, Śrī ŚrīŚacīnandana and Nitāichand. All those who attended had *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* and had a very enjoyable evening. About forty-five youths were able to use their abundant energy in a productive manner and learn about Kṛṣṇa consciousness in an enjoyable but educational way.

This year, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we were also able to perform Gītā Jayantī at three locations — Coventry, Rugby, and South London. In this way we were able to attract many people new to Kṛṣṇa consciousness and distribute books to them, thereby placing your literature before many newly interested people.

In February of this year Hari dāsa Prabhu was able to take a contingent of the youth to Bhārata to visit the holy *dhāmas* and see Kṛṣṇa consciousness being practiced by whole communities as a way of life in Māyāpur and Vṛndāvana. Nineteen inexperienced people, most of them under twenty-one, went to Bhārata. Nineteen experienced, fully committed adults emerged at the end of the trip. By all accounts their behavior had been exemplary, a credit to the name of ISKCON. Not only do we have a feeling of unity, greater cooperation by devotees, and a more significant understanding of Lord Caitanya’s movement, but we really, really believe in you and thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for bringing this mission to the West and allowing us, the miserable suffering people of the Western world, to join in the greatest experience ever. Śrīla Prabhupāda, from all the spirit souls in Coventry and surrounding areas, we love you.

Your devotees in Coventry, UK.  
(written by Daśaratha dāsa)

## Dallas

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.”**

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

**“I offer my humble obeisances unto the guru, the deliverer of the poor and helpless. By his mercy even a dumb man can become the greatest orator and a lame man can cross mountains.”**

*“Show Your Love for Me by How You Cooperate.”*

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept the most prostrated *sarvāṅga-daṇḍavat praṇāmas* that our hearts can command at the dust of Your Divine Grace’s most magnanimous and divine lotus feet, on this the most auspicious and holy occasion of your 109th Vyāsa-pūjā celebration.

All glories to Your Divine Grace, and all glories to your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, and all glories to your ISKCON, all nondifferent yet full of variegatedness.

As our most loving father, grandfather, and great-grandfather, please accept the following heartfelt, albeit puny and simple, offering of services as an expression of our deep love and appreciation for you.

Your followers here took the year following your 108th appearance anniversary in September 2004 as an opportunity to make some significant strides in pushing forward your magnanimous mission. We came together to discuss how we could please you the most, and in our discussions a number of factors emerged as the most important initiatives.

The very first principle that emerged was that we could show our love for you by the extent to which we cooperate with one another in pushing forth your mission. The next very important principle was that we should make every effort possible to take care of the devotees, so that, united, we can best practice *sādhana-bhakti* and preach with all our hearts.

Our community agreed on fifteen initiatives to effectively realize the above principles. Of these, we agreed to emphasize five areas of service as our immediate priority, namely, preaching services, youth services, communications services, resources services, and Deity services.

In the preaching services area, emphasis has been placed on dynamic college programs, innovative home programs, an improved Sunday Feast program, a Saturday night social program for newcomers, and proper reception of your guests at the temple.

In the youth services area, our goal has been to positively integrate our youth into every aspect of our community, so that they feel totally enlivened to participate and, indeed, to provide leadership in your mission.

In the communication services area, we have been actively pursuing ways and means of improving information dissemination via informative newsletters, a comprehensive website, effective media relations, and a weekly highlight bulletin for the devotees in our community.

In the resources services area, the focus has been on generating more financial, volunteer, and property resources, ensuring integrity at every stage of the handling of these resources, and guaranteeing proper financial reporting.

In the Deity services area, our target has been to increase the pleasure of our Deities with more elaborate worship, thereby enlivening everyone more and more.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we want our cooperation to bring about real unity. The essence of this Kṛṣṇa conscious principle is perfectly expressed in the following very important verse from the *ŚrīŚikṣāṣṭaka* prayers:

*tṛṇād api sunīcena taror iva sahiṣṇunā*  
*amāninā mānadena kīrtanīyaḥ sadā hariḥ*

**“One should chant the holy name of the Lord in a humble state of mind, thinking oneself lower than the straw in the street; one should be more tolerant than a tree, devoid of all sense of false prestige, and should be ready to offer all respect to others. In such a state of mind, one can chant the holy name of the Lord constantly.”**

We are striving to be really sincere and humble, to be eager to cooperate, eager to serve, eager to uplift, eager to encourage, and eager to sacrifice for the benefit of other devotees. We do not want to be judgmental of others; rather, we want to be free from offenses. We want to refrain from actions that create confusion, rivalry, conflict, and deviation from your teachings. Please bless us so that we may enthusiastically and happily hear and chant in the association of devotees and become free from lethargy and the desire for profit, adoration, and distinction. The cornerstone of our program will be *jīva-dayā* (practicing sincere compassion toward all), *nāma-ruci* (cultivating a real taste for the chanting of the holy names), and *vaiṣṇava-sevā* (serving the Vaiṣṇavas). We are cultivating a spirit of appreciation for every devotee, and a spirit of being gracious to all.

Please bless our efforts so that our tiny sparks of sincerity and enthusiasm may be fanned into a blazing fire of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Your mercy brings about true purity, which is the force that makes everything worthwhile possible. Your authority, expressed most eloquently in your books, is the basis of our endeavors; you are our supreme *śikṣā-guru,* creating all utility, the principle of all our activities; and we accept *saṅkīrtana* as the essence of your mission.

We thank you *ananta-koṭi* times, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are more dear than our own lives. Please bless us that we may always please you and never displease you.

One hundred eight Śrī Śrīmad Śrīla A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda-*kī jaya,* eternally!

Your servants, and the servants of your servants, and their servants, at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālachandjī Dhāma, Dallas, Texas, USA.  
(written by Nityānanda dāsa Adhikārī)

## Detroit

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, all the devotees at Devasadan Mandir are begging for your continuous mercy on all of us so that we can continue participating in the service of you and your most beloved Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjabihārījī. Remembering your most important instruction of cooperating among ourselves and working together, we have taken on many tasks to maintain and increase the glories of the Devasadan Mandir and Bhaktivedanta Cultural Center at the Fisher Mansion.

The biggest task for us was to replace the tiled roof. We had some special help in this regard. Because of the spread of your glories all around the world and the hard work of your many disciples for years, the chairman of the Motor City Heritage Foundation, just after visiting India, came to the temple looking for shelter and relief. He enjoyed *prasādam* with the devotees, a tour of the temple, and *darśana* of the most beautiful Deities. After observing the water damage from the roof, he offered to help and sent his secretary to assist Śrīmān Juṣya Prabhu to write the grant. And this was just the beginning of the shower of your mercy. He even added a special grant to print the new brochures for tours of the Bhaktivedanta Cultural Center at the Fisher Mansion.

Encouraged by this jump-start, we approached all our well-wishers and patrons for matching the grant. The first to come forward was your very dear disciple Śrīmān Ambarīṣa Prabhu and his wife Śrīmatī Swāhā-devī dāsī. Following their footsteps, many other dedicated souls participated in many ways. Now all the critical areas of the roof are replaced. Still, we want to continue to replace the whole roof to secure the building. Just recently, a very sincere and humble devotee, Mr. Dilip Patel, came to us offering all the funds to purchase the new tiles required for the roof. Unless you had inspired these souls, how would they have come forward to participate in pure loving devotional service to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjabihārījī?

Our Sunday Feast program is becoming more and more popular under the leadership of the Sriman Prasher family. As a result, now we have ecstatic *kīrtanas* during the 4:15 pm and 7:00 pm *āratis*. More and more people are enjoying the sumptuous *prasādam* served at the Sunday Feast program. The Ratha-yātrā team, under the leadership of Śrīmān Mādhava dāsa Prabhu, installed a new state-of-the-art sound system in the temple. So now everyone can hear your *bhajans* twenty-four hours a day inside and outside the temple. Our youth team is participating in many different projects. They are our showcase for the future of ISKCON. They enjoy *kīrtana, bhajans,* and *prasādam,* as well as cleaning the pots, gardening, and general maintenance of the temple.

Now we are praying so that we can not only finish the roof but also paint the building inside and out, restore the gardens and fountains, upgrade the electrical and heating systems, invite many more guests, and distribute your *prasādam* and books. Śrīmān Puruṣa Sūkta Prabhu is conducting successful preaching programs on the university campus in Ann Arbor, so we have more student participation. Bhakti Vṛkṣa devotees are coming forward to take more and more responsibilities in many parts of our project.

We have only one fear: that one of your dearmost frontline generals, His Holiness Bhakti Tīrtha Mahārāja, is preparing to join you and your mission away from this planet. He has been the greatest source of guidance and spiritual strength for all of us. We offer our humble prayers to you to do the needful, as you desire, because we do not know how to handle such a delicate situation.

Your humble servants at the Devasadan Mandir, Detroit, Michigan, USA.  
(written by Jagad-Guru dāsa)

## Eger

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your divine appearance day!

Your Vyāsa-pūjā day, the anniversary of your appearance, is a very auspicious opportunity for us to glorify you. Although you never came to Hungary in your physical form to start the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, you sent your sincere followers to do so on your behalf. In your books you explain that there are two kinds of association with the spiritual master, namely *vāṇī-sevā* and *vapu-sevā*. You always emphasized *vāṇī-sevā* — there was a limit to association with you in your physical presence. Nevertheless, we have seen in Hungary that by following your instructions we can get your personal association. We can undoubtedly experience your association through your mercy. It is your mercy alone that allows us to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness successfully and to please the *guru-paramparā* and Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Here in Eger we are trying to spread the *saṅkīrtana* movement through many kinds of activities.

• We are going out every day to distribute your books.

• We are distributing *prasādam*. We have a small restaurant where we offer nice *prasādam* to the many guests visiting us each day. And we are feeding the needy, too, through our Food for Life program.

• Twice a month we are holding open programs for students in the local university.

• We have a very good relationship with the leaders of our city, and we are building up new relationships as well.

• We are taking part in many of the city’s cultural programs.

• For many years we have been organizing *padayātrās* in different parts of Hungary, reaching many remote villages, giving the holy name and your books to many people.

• We are trying to expand the congregational preaching and engage the members of the congregation in more and more devotional service.

• Our plan is to open a new preaching center where the citizens of our town can learn about Kṛṣṇa consciousness through many different activities.

We would like to thank you for giving us the chance to preach. By that preaching we hope to please Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Please continue to give us your blessings and your mercy so that we can carry on your mission in Hungary, and specifically in Eger.

Your servants in Eger, Hungary.

## Ekaterinburg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, please accept our respectful obeisances and congratulations from the devotees of the Ekaterinburg *yātrā,* Russia.

The day of your divine appearance is the most joyful festival for ISKCON devotees. On this day we remember your life, which has become the example of spiritual success. We discuss your instructions year by year, and understand their strength and essence deeper and deeper.

On this day we are praying to you to take us unto your lotus feet — we are praying for your mercy. Without your mercy we can never get the mercy of Lord Caitanya and enter into the divine abode of the Lord’s eternal pastimes.

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi*

We have firm faith in your instructions. Your Vaiṣṇava community is wonderful and is spreading all over the world. Your divine books attract many people to the Vedic knowledge. People from around world visit Māyāpur and Vṛndāvana *dhāmas* and get wonderful association of your sincere followers and disciples.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this auspicious day of your divine appearance we pray for your mercy. Please help us return to our natural state of love and bliss!

All glories unto you!!

Your devotees in Ekaterinburg, Russia.

## Gainesville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Another year has come and gone,  
passing swiftly as life plays its song.  
Nearing the age when you arrived,  
we are feeling ourselves just barely alive.  
How’d you give Kṛṣṇa to the world at your age,  
to passionate souls whose lives were enraged?  
So lost to the glitter of Māyās sharp lure,  
we thought we were happy and life was secure.  
But you came to us like an ancient sage,  
with sharpened words that rattled our cage.  
You gave a new meaning to this senseless place;  
your words for us were a fiery mace,  
Penetrating deeply with your morning classes  
about men like hogs, dogs, camels, and asses.  
That painful truth ran cold through our hearts,  
piercing our illusions like spiritual darts.  
As we chanted and followed the rules that you gave,  
our senses no longer our minds enslaved.  
We stood up like soldiers and struggled to fight  
all our old habits, the creatures of dark nights.  
It’s forty years since you arrived in this place  
to fill our souls with your kindness and grace.  
We know for certain it is your strength alone  
that keeps us all heading our way back to Home.  
We will succeed if we stick to your teachings,  
pressing forward and always preaching.  
We thank you deeply on your appearance day,  
praying you will keep us from going astray.  
We fall at your feet and pray to stay  
your eternal servants for the rest of our days.

Your insignificant servants in Gainesville, Florida, USA.

## Gaṅgā Sāgara Baru

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your causeless mercy we have acquired 122 hectares of land, which we are now trying to develop into a farm. Our long-term goal is to develop this land into a Vaiṣṇava village. The land is in a very remote place — Kapuas District, Central Borneo, Indonesia — and we have named it Gaṅgā Sāgara Baru (New Gaṅgā Sāgara). Now we are in the very beginning stage, with only two *gṛhastha* couples staying full time there. Please be merciful to us so that we will always be inspired and enthusiastic to develop this farm project.

Thank you again for allowing us to participate in your mission by trying to set up a community based on the principle of simple living and high thinking.

Your humble servants at Gaṅgā Sāgara Baru Farm, Lupak Village, Central Kalimantan, Indonesia.

## Ghana

Dear Jagad Guru Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please kindly accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to your divine mission on this earthly planet! On this most auspicious day of your holy appearance, it behooves all who have somehow or other received your causeless mercy to loudly proclaim your unlimited glories, or at least register our appreciation for your transcendental descent into the material world.

By your unlimited and causeless mercy I was blessed to be on Jayapatāka Mahārāja’s 2005 Gaṅgā Safari, and this gave me the unlimited opportunity to appreciate you all the more.

On this safari, which was just one of many held so far, there were a hundred and fifty devotees from forty countries around the world, traveling as one big transcendental family from Māyāpur to Hrisikesh/Haridwar. What was spectacular about this safari was that it was a wonderful preaching opportunity for attracting everyone to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Whichever city, town, or village the safari descended on, all attention went to the devotees. Hearing the *mahā-mantra* being vibrated so enthusiastically, even the impersonalists, the yogis, and the many *bhogīs* who abound in India joined in the chanting.

If you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, had not descended on this auspicious day 109 years ago to give us all this rare opportunity, how could there be such a transcendental safari? Śrīla Prabhupāda, you deserve a hundred Nobel Prizes for creating the real United Nations. Politicians who have no clue as to what real unity is have failed woefully, despite the volumes of resources at their disposal. But with your Bhaktivedanta purports you have created a world organization not only for uniting humanity but, most importantly, for delivering all from the cycle of repeated birth and death.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, some of your critics say you did not teach your followers everything about love of Godhead. But I challenge such critics to read just one purport of your glorious *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* (at Chapter 4, Text 10) and reconsider their statements. You have been empowered by Lord Kṛṣṇa to give everything necessary in your Bhaktivedanta purports for going back to Godhead. Śrīla Prabhupāda, who would have believed that *yavanas* and *mlecchas* from Africa could have taken to Kṛṣṇa consciousness, what to speak of the renounced order of life! But just by your causeless mercy and desire to give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to everyone, irrespective of color, creed, and location, the first native African, in the person of Bhakti Dhīra Dāmodara Swami, took the vows of renunciation in Śrīdhām Māyāpur on the holy appearance day of Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu. This is a manifestation of your unbiased kindness and mercy.

Though we are deeply pained at heart to lose the physical association of your great general His Holiness Bhakti Tīrtha Swami Mahārāja, who has been at the forefront of the African preaching for many years, we take consolation in the fact that he has over the years laid a solid foundation for the African preaching, especially in West Africa. And now, by his untiring efforts, everything is being managed by the local devotees, which Your Divine Grace so much desired. On this most auspicious day of your divine appearance, we would humbly request Your Divine Grace to bless the African preaching with the association of more fatherly senior devotees who can inspire us so that our preaching can grow from strength to strength.

Your most insignificant granddisciples in Ghana.  
(written by Śrīvāsa dāsa)

## Gianyar

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

In 1998 the devotees at Gianyar, Bali, agreed to purchase a .08 hectare plot of land, which was put under your name. We named the temple that rose on that land the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava Mandir. The devotees also purchased some land adjoining the temple property, with the hope of developing a Vaiṣṇava village. At present we have acquired .9 hectare, or close to 2.5 acres.

With a generous financial contribution from Śrīmān Satyanārāyaṇa Prabhu, we now have a permanent temple. Eight devotees are staying there full time, serving the deities of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava, Śrī Lalitā, Śrī Viśākhā, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and Śrī Narasiṁha. We have about two hundred congregational members, among whom seven families are building their house near the temple. We hope that in the future more and more devotees will also build their homes nearby.

We have a regular Deity worship program, with *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class as usual, and we also hold a regular Sunday Feast program for guests. We have also started a small store, where we sell books, *prasādam,* incense, stickers, and so on. By your grace we also run a Sunday *gurukula* program for devotee children, with three of our devotees acting as teachers.

Our plan is to build a more permanent *brahmacārī* ashram and guesthouse. We are also planning to build a path around the site for conducting Ratha-yātrā festivals. Another plan is to develop a small farm to provide the necessities of the temple. We hope that in the future our Gaura Hari Foundation will be able to print your books and distribute them widely to the Indonesian people.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your kind mercy upon us.

Your servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava Mandir in Gianyar, Bali, Indonesia.  
(written by Anuttama dāsa)

## Gītā-nāgarī Baru

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, saint of saints, who are the well-wisher of all living entities,

Please accept my humble obeisances at your lotus feet. On this day of your appearance anniversary, I would like to recall some of your important instructions.

O my dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please be merciful unto me and all the devotees serving at the Gītā Nāgarī Baru village project here in Lampung, so that we can fulfill your desire to protect the cows, maintain and cultivate the land, and do everything we need to do. We should not depend on others, nor work on the outside. In this way we can practice simple living and high thinking. Many times His Holiness Bhakti Rāghava Swami and Śrīmān Gaura Maṇḍala Bhūmi Prabhu have stressed this simple life, encouraging us to eat what we have planted and do devotional service so that our lives will become perfect.

O supreme *gurudeva*! You are the direct manifestation of Lord Caitanya’s mercy. You have also instructed us that cooperating among ourselves will prove our love for you. It is *māyā*if we fight, and you have said this dissension is more dangerous than demons fighting us. Let us keep this instruction in our hearts and actions. Let us be honest and humble with each other.

In this way we can build Gītā Nāgarī Baru village and live peacefully, naturally, and self-sufficiently.

O my dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have shown us how we can live a peaceful life and how to experience the highest taste of *kṛṣṇa-prema* by awakening our Kṛṣṇa consciousness through chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*. O my lord, we are praying at your lotus feet to keep us engaged always as we are now.

O divine master, you displayed the symptoms of a perfect Vaiṣṇava differently at different times.

Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura instructs us as follows :

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi*  
*dhyāyan stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri-sandhyaṁ*  
*vande guroḥśrī-caraṇāravindam*

**By satisfying the spiritual master, the Supreme Personality of Godhead becomes satisfied, but by not satisfying the spiritual master, one has no chance of being promoted to the plane of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.**

It is our great good fortune that you kindly allowed us to take shelter of our spiritual master to assist you in your divine mission, spreading the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Please give us your mercy so that we can follow our spiritual master’s instructions, including his instructions to plant vegetables and rice, protect the cows, and live simply, not using chemicals and not depending on outsiders. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please gives us your mercy. Then our spiritual lives will be successful, and both you and Kṛṣṇa will become happy and satisfied. In this way we can progress in devotional service and go back to Goloka Vṛndāvana, where there is no anxiety, as there is in this material world.

O Jagat Guru, O Ācāryadeva, on this Vyāsa-pūjā day we pray to you, and to our spiritual master, to please keep us always engaged in your devotional service. Please help us remember and follow all your instructions, the best way to get the mercy of our spiritual mater. Now we are begging you, please give us the strength to deserve this great fortune. Please keep us as particles of dust at your lotus feet. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

The devotees at the Gītā Nāgarī Baru Vedic farm project in Lampung, Indonesia.  
(written by Ṣaḍbhuja Gaurāṅga dāsa)

## Goteborg

Goteborg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, our beloved spiritual grandfather,

Today, on this most auspicious 109th anniversary of your appearance day, please accept our most humble obeisances unto your divine lotus feet. We, the devotees in Goteborg, wish to express our deep gratitude for what you did for us. What you did has no comparison, and your compassion toward the fallen souls has no limit.

As time goes by, we get more realizations of your greatness and your love toward all living beings. Your surrender to your spiritual master and Śrī Kṛṣṇa made all your efforts successful in a way that surprised even you. If we can follow your example and surrender in a similar way, we will, by the mercy coming through the disciplic succession, also be successful in our spiritual lives.

The most wonderful thing is that you took this high philosophy and expressed it in such a way that Westerners could actually grasp it. No one had ever been able to accomplish that before! And because of your mercy, we thereby came to understand what sinful and useless lives we were living. By giving us knowledge about the eternality of the soul, and giving us hope that at the end of our lives we could actually go back to Godhead and there become a servant of a servant of Lord Kṛṣṇa, we were inspired to take up this sublime process of *bhakti-yoga*.

Śrīla Prabhupada, you certainly played the most important roll in our lives. Because you saved us from material sufferings and future hellish conditions and put us in contact with the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, we are forever thankful and indebted to you.

As we see Kali-yuga rapidly increasing, we beg you to please help us chant the holy names of the Lord more seriously and preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in a better way. We are all dependent on your mercy, so please continue to guide us in our daily lives and services.

Your granddisciples in Goteborg, Sweden.

## Guadalajara

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our most humbles obeisances under the dust of your divine lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, since you departed from this material world, we have seen how your *śakti* has manifested in various ways: there are more temples, devotees, books being distributed, *prasādam* distribution programs, etc. When you disappeared from this world, some persons thought your great spiritual movement would be finished very soon, but everyone is witnessing that each day your ISKCON is growing more and more due to your incredible *śakti*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can see how your masterpiece (ISKCON) is now working very nicely, given spiritual knowledge, shelter, and food to all kinds of people around the world. But the blind, faithless, and envious feel that ISKCON is bad.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your are very dear to Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu because you had the courage to come to the Western countries to save all of us and fulfil His prophecy that in every town and village His holy name would be chanted.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, since you are so kind, please give us useless souls your causeless mercy. Because we are very low, fallen, and without intelligence, we are in great need of your divine guidance and mercy to stick to our spiritual lives. But if you are not merciful to us, it is sure that we will again try to get pleasure from material life. So please protect these devotees of yours.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are very grateful to you for the great matchless gift of wonderful Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Thank you so much, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your most fallen aspiring servants in New Nīlācala-dhāma, Guadalajara, Mexico.  
(written by Premānanda dāsa)

## Houston

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

We can never appreciate enough your coming to the West and bringing forth this *bhakti-yoga* and making arrangements so that each one of us is able to get Vaiṣṇava association. You have explained that this factor — Vaiṣṇava association — is the most important factor in us becoming and staying a Vaiṣṇava.

We can never repay our debt to you but can only express our love and try to make this *bhakti-yoga* available to others through *saṅkīrtana*.

Your servants at Hare Krishna Dhām, Houston, Texas, USA.

## Karuna Bhavan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to you, our Prabhupāda, at whose feet all other masters take shelter.

This year I have been asked to write the international offering to you on behalf of the Scottish Yātrā. How do I correctly and precisely represent the mood of surrender and service of your family here, who have always been taught to work as a unit to please you? Please forgive me for misrepresenting or overlooking any of what they do for you.

There is so much to glorify you for — what you have done, what you set up for all of us to be able to serve in your mission, and so on. I have been allowed to perform the service of helping to care for our Deities here, Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Khoda-Nitāi–Māyāpura-śaśi. So much has and is being done for Their worship; the *pūjās* are kept simple so they can and will always be maintained. Śrīmatī Vṛndādevī and Śrī Giridhārī-lāl also reside here with us. Twice a day all Their Lordships are offered sweets as an offering between meals. This engages our minds and bodies in serving Them more. Vṛndādevī is very beautiful. She stays in her own room with Girirāj and her parrots. She is worshiped by one of your *gurukula* girls, a fact I am sure is most pleasing to you. This girl also designs, embroiders, and sews Śrīmatī Vṛndādevīs clothes.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in the last few weeks at least thirty new outfits have been designed for the Deities. We have all the cloth ready to be sewn. Some outfits will have braiding, others embroidery; all are comfortable to wear and made by us. We also have outfits made from tartan (the Scottish cloth). Many Scottish people who see our Deities are very pleased to see Them wearing this cloth. You taught us that we should not see the Deity forms of the Lord as stones or statues, but that we should see Them as forms of the Supreme Lord Himself, and that we must serve and worship Them as such. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for sending us one of your devotees to direct us in the service of Their Lordships. We have been shown how to be personal with our Lords, to think of Their comfort, to dress Them not for show but for Their pleasure, in clothes that are not too big, too small, or too heavy. Śrī Śrī Khoda-Nitāi–Māyāpura-śaśi are always dressed differently. “How to be personal with Their Lordships once again.” By our dressing Them differently, our minds become more absorbed. When we see Them, design and sew outfits for Them, or dress Them, we have to think of Them more. They are not two dolls but Gaura and Nitāi, the two brothers who came to deliver the fallen souls. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us this wonderful way to serve the Lord. Please help us always be absorbed in the worship of the devotees and Their Lordships.

Millions of people are receiving Lord Gaurāṅga’s name every day. Modern technology like computers and phones are being used to create a weblike network throughout the world so that Lord Gaura’s name will become a household word. Now people from all over the world are engaged in discussions, conferences, debates, and arguments based on Lord Gaurāṅga’s name. People who are chanting “Gaurāṅga” are now preaching to others: “Try it, it works for me.” Thank you again, Śrīla Prabhupāda. These people are chanting Gaura’s name. They don’t know why, they don’t know what for, but by the Lord’s mercy they are becoming blissful and peaceful. So many people receive “Gaurāṅga” on the street from our *saṅkīrtana* devotees every day — Gaurāṅga CD’S, pens, stressballs, combs, stickers, and posters, and also Gaurāṅga rock. These are only some of the ways that Lord Gaurāṅga is getting into everyone’s home.

Thank you again and again, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us so much, for sending us one of your own disciples, whose desire is to give everyone the holy name and who has not given up on us, because everything is done for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Please help us see more and more ways to please you and the *paramparā*.

Your servants in Karuṇā Bhavan, the Scottish Yātrā, UK.

## Kecskemet

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances! All glories to you! All glories to Śrī Śrī Dayāl-Nitāi–Vijay-Gaurāṅga! All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara!

On your appearance day we would like to honor and glorify you with the following words.

Two years ago the devotees and congregational members in Kecskemet rented a nice house at the perimeter of the city and turned it into a wonderful Hare Kṛṣṇa temple housing Śrī Pañca-tattva. By your mercy, over the last few years we have seen some positive changes here. The people have slowly forgotten their fear of our activities. They no longer believe that we kidnap young boys and girls for irreligious reasons, but rather they consider us very positively. The newspapers also give nice reports on our activities, such as Food for Life and festivals.

In the last six years, with the leadership of Dāmodara Prabhu and Dharmiṣṭhā Mātājī, who plan our main preaching programs very enthusiastically and with great expertise, we have tried to saturate the whole city with Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Mainly we want to be very simple, open-minded, and straightforward in our preaching activities, and our hope is in the power of the holy name. When you arrived in New York, the main city of America, you began the same system — to sit down somewhere and chant the *mahā-mantra* so everyone could hear. By your faith and devotion and the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and because you spoke so straightforwardly with determination, so many young people came to you, and very soon they surrendered unto your lotus feet. As the Kali-yuga gets worse and worse, more and more people realize the miseries of life. In your classes you stressed several simple themes: we are not the body but spirit souls, and our only goal should be to awaken our dormant Kṛṣṇa consciousness and return to the spiritual world to serve the Divine Couple, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, with love.

We follow the same system here in Kecskemet, and it seems that you like it, because you send so many kind, open-minded souls to us. We realized that if we don’t make any compromise but straightforwardly speak the truth about materialism, the main motivating force of the present human society, then the young people will react very positively. In contrast with so many other religions, we don’t want to make a deal: “OK, just have faith in God. You can watch television, buy anything in the shops, eat whatever you like, and have a new girlfriend every month, and at the end God will call you with satisfaction, if only you have faith in Him.” We have conversations with representatives of other religions about the present situation, and they often say, “We can’t do anything. We may be very kind to them, but young people don’t want to be religious.”

We have a different experience. Not only young people but elders come to us, stay with us, and take up the chanting of the holy name — that wonderful and mystical process. As their hearts begin to be cleansed a little bit, they take part in *kīrtanas, bhajans,* and classes with great enthusiasm. They do more and more voluntary service.

We would like to thank you for making all this possible with your own example, personal advice, and books. Of course, we know that we will meet with difficulties in our lives — as you did — but we have faith in the formula: chanting the holy name together, honoring and distributing nice *prasādam,* distributing your books, and in general always preaching and spreading the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. By this means, according to your promise, not only will we enter into the personal association of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, but we can save many thousands of spirit souls in Kecskemet and the surrounding area. What else do we need? So many people speak about their mystical realizations — *siddha-deha* and this and that. Maybe such things are right for them, but we are just happy in this simple way. This way of life is enough for us, and we are grateful to you for it. As a sign of our gratitude, we promise that we will never stop spreading your mission and will try throughout our whole lives to introduce the holy name in every town and village.

Your eternal servants in Kecskemet, Hungary.

## Kiev

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this blessed day, please accept our obeisances at your lotus feet.

On this day each one of your disciples and followers tries to express his gratitude and offer some present to you. On this day everyone wants to glorify you and force his way forward closer to you, desiring to tell you about service rendered for you.

You have given us so much and have obliged us so much. You have left for us your love and your image, which is nondifferent from you and which is you yourself.

In our temple we especially feel your presence, and in that special atmosphere we experience a simultaneous realization of our own lack of qualifications and your perpetual compassion, kindness, and love for Kṛṣṇa. This message of love is being passed through the chain of *paramparā,* and we received it and continue to receive it from you, our spiritual master and ISKCON’s founder-*ācārya*.

You have left a special gift for this world, the gift of transcendental knowledge and the power of pure love, which lifts up all living entities from the quagmire of ignorance and suffering to the realm of happiness, care, and warm loving relationships with the Lord and His devotees, who are all fully convinced that “We are eternal souls, servants of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Lord, the source of love and joy. His service is the way to enlightenment and perfection.”

“Prabhupāda’s House”— this is the title of the newsletter of the Kiev community. Prabhupāda’s House is the house where there is a place for everyone, where everyone will be warmly welcomed and happy, where everyone will find what he has been looking for for millions of lifetimes.

You have shown how everyone can be happy: through Kṛṣṇa consciousness. By this process one will realize that he is not alone in an empty world, that his life is full of purpose, that he has a role to play in Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement, and that the price for attaining love of God is not insurmountably high.
